# Demon's Souls Weapons & Shields Reference

> **Source:** [http://demonssouls.wikidot.com/weapons](http://demonssouls.wikidot.com/weapons)
>
> This document contains detailed information for all weapons and shields in Demon's Souls,
> including characteristics, special effects, **durability**, weight, stat requirements,
> and exhaustive upgrade paths with per-level stats.

## How to Read Weapon Attributes

| Attribute | Description |
|-----------|-------------|
| **Damage** | Format: `Physical / Magic / Fire`. Some weapons also list `Bleed`, `Poison`, or `Plague` buildup, and damage type(s) in parentheses: `(Normal)`, `(Blunt)`, `(Slashing)`, `(Piercing)`. |
| **Durability** | The durability of the weapon. Effectiveness severely deteriorates when durability falls below 30%. **High priority for save editing.** |
| **Weight** | The weight of the weapon. Carrying over 50% of Equip Burden reduces roll speed; over 100% reduces movement to walking. |
| **Stats Needed** | Format: `STR / DEX / MAG / FTH`. Wielding without meeting STR/DEX requirements incurs a damage penalty. Two-handing gives a 50% bonus to STR. |
| **Stat Bonuses** | Rating from S (best) to E (worst), or `-` for no bonus. Format: `STR / DEX / MAG / FTH`. |
| **Damage Reduction %** | Format: `Physical / Magic / Fire`. How much damage the weapon mitigates while blocking. |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | Determines stamina expended to block. Higher = better at blocking strong attacks without guard breaking. |
| **Range** | (Bows/Crossbows only) Effective range of the weapon. |
| **Magic/Miracle Adjustment** | (Catalysts/Talismans only) Base magic/miracle power used in spell damage calculations. |

### Upgrade Path Legend

| Path | Material | Starts At | Max Level | Special Effect |
|------|----------|-----------|-----------|---------------|
| **Basic** | Hardstone / Sharpstone | +0 | +10 | Increases base physical damage |
| **Sharp** | Bladestone | +0 | +5 | Increases DEX bonus, lowers STR bonus |
| **Crushing** | Greystone | +0 or +3 | +5 | Increases STR bonus, removes DEX bonus |
| **Quality** | Clearstone | +3 | +5 | Balanced STR+DEX scaling |
| **Mercury** | Mercurystone | +3 | +5 | Adds poison effect |
| **Fatal** | Marrowstone | +3 | +5 | Adds critical (backstab/riposte) damage; lowers base damage |
| **Crescent** | Darkmoonstone | +6 | +5 | Adds magic damage + MP regen; scales with MAG; eliminates STR/DEX bonuses |
| **Moon** | Moonlightstone | +6 | +5 | Adds magic damage; scales with MAG |
| **Blessed** | Faintstone | +6 | +5 | Adds magic damage + HP regen; scales with FTH; lowers STR/DEX bonuses |
| **Dragon** | Dragonstone | +3 | +5 | Adds fire damage; removes all stat bonuses |
| **Tearing** | Suckerstone | +0 or +3 | +5 | Adds bleed effect; increases DEX, lowers STR |
| **Sticky** | Spiderstone | +3 | +5 | Bows only (not crossbows); greatly increases DEX, lowers STR; +5 range at +1 |
| **Dark** | Cloudstone | +6 | +5 | Shields only; raises Magic Damage Reduction %; resets Guard Break Reduction |
| **Colorless** | Colorless Demon's Soul | — | +5 | For special/unique weapons only |
| **Demon's Soul** | Specific Demon's Soul | — | varies | Creates special boss weapons |

## Daggers

_7 weapons_

#### Dagger

> A standard dagger. A slashing weapon that inflicts heavy damage on soft flesh but hasa (sic) a limited effect on metal armor and hard scales. Daggers are very effective after parrying, as well as in close-range stabs from behind.

#### Parrying Dagger

> A dagger made for parrying. Normally equipped in the left hand. It is the weapon used by those of a masked society who meet in secret to discuss the harnessing of Souls. Women of this order have become adept in using this unique dagger in place of a heavier shield.

#### Kris Blade

> A small ceremonial sword. Old runes are expertly chiseled into the blade. Used as an aid for incantations. The wielder's magic ability increases, though he or she will suffer more magical damage.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 20/62/0 (Normal) (Piercing) |
| **Durability** | **120** |
| **Weight** | 1.0 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 8/0/16/0 D/E/B/- |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 50/10 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 35 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

The Kris Blade increases Magic spell damage, but lowers Magic defense Effect stacks with Ring of Magical Sharpness and Monk's Head Collar Magic damage increase does not affect the God's Wrath miracle or magic damage from weapons In use, the Kris Blade generates the same aura as the Ring of Magical Sharpness

| **Durability** | **240** |
| **Weight** | 6.0 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 221/16/0/0 E/C/-/- |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

Other than looking great, it has no desirable qualities. You'll find other weapons that have both a higher base damage and a greater contribution from your stats. The damage increase for its thrusting attack is substantially more than the normal boost for such an attack, which shows the "penetrating" feature of the weapon, so use that to judge it at its best. Also, this sword has a great reach, giving it an advantage over other weapons with a shorter range.

#### Storm Ruler

> A large sword with a thorny, spiraled blade. Now abandoned and most of it's power lost, it seems that it once dominated foes, but in an area with ancestral spirits or a monolith forest, you may be able to see it's past sky-cutting power.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 150/0/0 (Normal) |
| **Durability** | **10** |
| **Weight** | 6.0 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 0/0/0/0 -/-/-/- |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 60.0/10.0 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 45 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

The Storm Ruler is a unique sword that does not do very high damage and has an extremely low durability, so it breaks easily. It does, however, have a knockdown effect on all horizontal swings that connect. In pvp, this weapon is often times used as a tool to knockdown opponents for performing a follow-up action (e.g. use recovery items, cast spells). It has the lowest durability of any weapon (any item for that matter) in the game. When used in the final boss zone of Shrine of Storms it has a special attack unlocked which allows its attack to split the air with a thunder-crack, giving this melee weapon a vertical ranged attack. That special attack only works if the weapon is equipped in the right hand. It has the lowest stat requirements of any item in the game.

This sword is classified as a Large Sword and is one of two swords in the game that cannot be blocked by shields, the other being the Blind . The sword has magic reduction built in. The Large Sword of Moonlight +0 can be traded with Sparkly the Crow for a Ring of Devout Prayer 1

**Upgrade Paths:**

##### King's Field

The Moonlight Sword is a recurring weapon in The King's Field series of games. The series was also developed by From Software . Demon's Souls &amp; Dark Souls are the spiritual successors of these games.

Requires: King's Field 1 - this weapon is one of the end-game weapons from King's Field 1. The game can be completed without it, but it is very difficult. This game was not released outside of Japan, but translations into most major languages can now be found.*, King's Field 2 - this weapon is the main focus of King's Field 2. In the game, Prince Alexander's goal was to return the sword to King Alfred. King's Field 2 is known as King's Field in North America., King's Field 3 - The Large Sword Of Moonlight also appears as a very powerful, but unnecessary weapon in King's Field 2. King's Field 3 is known simply as King's Field 2 in North America., King's Field 4 is the title of the fourth and, as of yet, final installment in the King's Field series. Kings Field 4 is known simply as King's Field: The Ancient City in North America.

_Level data not available._

---

#### Morion Blade

> A gnarled, thorny large sword of black quartz. Forged from the soul of the Demon "Storm King," it is actually the marrow of the storm beasts. These beasts take pleasure in the wielder's peril, and in these situations, the blade's attack power is greatly increased.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 210/0/0 (Normal) |
| **Durability** | **400** |
| **Weight** | 3.0 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 141/14/0/0 -/-/-/- |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 60.0/10.0 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 45 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

This sword will increase any damage by 60% when your character's HP is less than 30%. This effect is identical to the Clever Rat's Ring's 50% increase, and can be stacked with it for a massive 140% damage boost (not 110%) The reason it's 140% is because the damage values are multiplicative e.g. 200 damage multiplied by the Clever Rat's Ring at 50% = 300 damage, the newly obtained figure of 300 is then multiplied by the Morion Blade 60% = 480 damage, this stacked with a Cursed Weapon produces devastating results. The Morion Blade's real value comes from this effect and has little use as a real weapon since the base damage is not that impressive and has no bonus from character stats. A single 'Dying ATK Up' effect will exceed any one bonus from a Kris Blade +5 , Ring of Magical Sharpness , or Monk's Head Collar for spells. However, both types of these effects can be combined for impressive spell damage. The HP loss needed to satisfy the 30% HP requirement is based on the max HP of your current form. Meaning that using the effect in Soul Form is much more risky. A Cling Ring will help in this regard, and reduces the likelihood of dying from minor damage. The glowing aura from the 'Dying ATK Up' effect, much like regeneration buffs, will reveal your position to players in PVP despite use of stealth effects. You will still receive the bonus power ups even if you don't meet the sword's requirements.

#### Dragon Bone Smasher

> A bladeless, thick mass of iron used for smashing. According to the ancestors of Stonefang's excavators, it's said this weapon was created to face dragons, and is imbued with magic to protect the wielder from fire.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 200/0/0 (Normal) |
| **Durability** | **600** |
| **Weight** | 20.0 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 301/0/0/0 B/-/-/- |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 70.0/10.0 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 45 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

Don't be fooled by its seemingly low B rank in strength. It also has high base damage, and almost all other weapons with S rankings have low base. This means the weapon will not require so much investment in its attribute to deal large amounts of damage. At 99 Strength, it has 280 + 280 Physical Attack. Keep in mind also that although you don't really notice it, the B rank will still increase to a higher bonus but will stay inside the B rating so it becomes hard to tell. Can be enchanted using Curse Weapon and Light Weapon If shown to Scirvir, The Wanderer the player will receive a Pure Greystone . This will not remove the sword from your inventory so feel free to show it to him (i.e. hold it in your right hand or left hand) without any consequences.

---

#### Soulbrandt

> The Soul-rending blade passed down through the Boletaria Royal Family. It is a black blade that forms a matching pair set with DemonBrand(t). After Allant rose to the throne, it was always in the king's hands. Old King Allant favored the blade for the way its power increased the more demonic the wielder's Soul became.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 120/130/0 (Normal) |
| **Durability** | **400** |
| **Weight** | 5.5 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 181/14/12/0 -/-/-/- |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 60.0/10 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 45 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

The Soulbrandt is a unique sword that increases your damage according to your Character Tendency. At Pure Black Character Tendency , the Soulbrandt has 120 + 120 Physical and 130 + 130 Magic attack power At White Character Tendency , the Soulbrandt has 0 attack power

---

#### Demonbrandt

> The demon-rending blade passed down through the Boletaria Royal Family. It is a white blade that forms a matching pair set with Soulbrand(t). It is particularly effective on demons. Old King Allant spurned the blade for the way its power increased the less demonic the wielder's Soul became.

| Stat | Value |

The Demonbrandt is a unique sword that increases your damage according to your Character Tendency . At Pure White Character Tendency , the Demonbrandt has 130 + 130 Physical and 110 + 110 Magic attack power At Pure Black Character Tendency , the Demonbrandt has 0 Physical and Magic attack power.

#### Northern Regalia

> Legacy of the Old Boletaria Kings. The shadow this sword casts is of both the Soulbrandt and the Demonbrandt. Little is known of its origin even in Boletarian Royal Family lore, but it is said to have been left in the world along with the Old One for malicious purposes.

#### Hiltless

> A hiltless uchigatana. It has a blade that seems fairly wet. It was forged by a famous swordsmith in a distant country, but since the wielder must grip the blade, it's extremely difficult to handle, causing damage to the wielder.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 121/0/0 Bleed 180 (Slashing) (Piercing) |
| **Durability** | **70** |
| **Weight** | 4.5 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 181/19/0/0 E/B/-/- |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 45.0/10.0 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 30 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

The Hiltless is one of two unique katanas in the game. Every hit that connects to your opponent (whether it be their body, shield, or a weapon being two handed) will drain 3% of your HP. If you swing and hit another surface (i.e. walls, breakable objects, etc.), it will not drain 3% of your HP. Also, Backstabs and Ripostes are exempt from harming you for 3% damage.

---

#### Blind

> A curved sword with a clear blade. Widely known along with the courage of the dull-gold knight, Selen Vinland, it is one of Vinland's treasures. The blade is illusory, and light as a feather. It can pass through a shield's defences.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 70/0/0 Bleed 120 (Slashing) |
| **Durability** | **50** |
| **Weight** | 0.1 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 3/24/0/0 -/S/-/- |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 10/10 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 25 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

A sword that ignores shields, it does normal damage to players even when they have their shields up. At max stats the Blind has 210 Physical Attack. However, be aware of the Blind's low durability rating of 50 points. It is the lightest weapon in the game.

---

#### Magic Sword 'Makoto'

> An ill-rumored katana mentioned in folklore, it is named for a giant in a distant country. When its unique, long blade meets skin, it tears it apart into a wound that is said will never heal. It is a cursed weapon that devours the wielder's life energy, but despite this, warriors continue to be drawn to this katana.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 210/0/0 Bleed 327 (Slashing) (Piercing) |
| **Durability** | **70** |
| **Weight** | 5.5 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 181/13/0/0 -/-/-/- |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 45.0/10.0 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 30 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

The Magic Sword "Makoto" is a unique katana that you are asked to retrieve by Satsuki . It is a cursed katana and will drain 1% of your life every two seconds when you have it out. The health drain can be offset by wearing a Regenerator's Ring , the Adjudicator's Shield or a Blessed Weapon . The sword can also be used as a tool to activate the Atk Up effect of the Clever Rat's Ring and the Morion Blade when health goes below 30%. Note that first HP drain happens the moment Makoto is equipped, so the process can be sped up by repeatedly switching between Makoto and any other weapon. The Magic Sword "Makoto" is a good starting sword but tends to be weaker once you reach later game play due to not having any added parameters. After a few new games, you may want to consider switching to a custom Uchigatana of choice or to the Hiltless because of its added parameters. However, Makoto is still useful for its 10 damage / second bleed (at +5) and slightly increased range. Despite its name, Magic Sword "Makoto" does not deal magic damage, only physical damage (slashing/piercing) Magic Sword "Makoto" will entirely drain the life of the player. If Second Chance is active and all health is lost from the player due to the sword, the character will be revived.

#### Epee Rapier

> A ceremonial rapier with intricate golden designs. It is a weapon favored by those of a masked society who meet in secret to discuss the harnessing of Souls. It is imbued with a strong, magical flame, which is one reason why these vicious people are feared.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 50/0/80 (Piercing) |
| **Durability** | **400** |
| **Weight** | 1.5 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 9/14/24/0 E/C/C/- |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 45.0/10.0 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 30 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

As a piercing sword weapon, it has the useful property of being able to attack while you keep a shield raised. Of all the piercing swords or Spears / Poles class items which all share this property, this is the only one which is enchanted with Fire damage (the others have no Upgrade: Dragon (Dragonstone) path). Additionally, a valuable and unique feature is that its Fire damage scales up with the users Magic stat, so it scales somewhat like a Upgrade: Moon (Moonlightstone) except with added Fire damage instead of Magic. At maximum Strength and Dexterity the Epee Rapier will do 50 + 40 Physical Damage and with maximum Magic, 80 + 144 Fire Damage.

#### Needle of Eternal Agony

> A large, curved needle with barbed stingers. This weapon was forged from the Soul of the Demon "Maneater." It stabs its target, hooks into the flesh, and slowly grinds away at the Soul. Could it symbolize the relationship between the host and snake within the Maneater?

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 10/50/0 (Piercing) |
| **Durability** | **400** |
| **Weight** | 0.5 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 6/20/20/0 -/-/-/- |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 40.0/10.0 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 25 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

This weapon will award the player +20 souls every time they hit an opponent, however the damage is a joke, so just make it for the valuable weapons trophy and toss it in storage. It is easier, more rewarding, and faster to farm souls from alternative methods listed on the wiki. As a side note, it can be somewhat useful if you die and need to repair your weapons before going back to your bloodstain, since it can steal souls from the Maiden in Black and the Monumental for some quick change. It's a common misconception that the weapon takes away or drains +20 souls from other players in PVP. However, this is not true and the opponent will always have the same amount of souls regardless of how many times you hit them. Also note that this weapon can not be enchanted.

##### Basic

The Battle Axe requires Hardstones (55 shards, 29 large shards, 14 chunks, and 1 pure) and 340 Souls per upgrade (3,400 total):

Requires: Highest Physical Attack., Greatest Attack Power if both Strength and Dexterity are less than 30.

_Level data not available._

##### Quality

The Quality Battle Axe requires a Battle Axe +3, Clearstones (25 shards, 9 chunks, and 1 pure), 510 Souls per upgrade (2,550 total), and 750 Souls to fully repair:

Requires: Highest Dexterity Bonus., Greatest Attack Power if both Strength and Dexterity are greater than 30.

_Level data not available._

##### Crushing

The Crushing Battle Axe requires a Battle Axe +3, Greystones (25 shards, 9 chunks, and 1 pure), and 340 Souls per upgrade (1,700 total):

Requires: Highest Strength Bonus., No Dexterity Bonus., Greatest Attack Power if Strength is greater than 30 and Dexterity is less than 20.

_Level data not available._

##### Dragon

The Dragon Battle Axe requires a Battle Axe +3, Dragonstones (25 shards, 9 chunks, and 1 pure), 580 Souls per upgrade (2,900 total), and 850 Souls to fully repair:

Requires: Adds Fire Attack and Fire Damage Reduction., No Strength or Dexterity Bonus., Greatest Attack Power if at weapon Requirement.

_Level data not available._

##### Moon

The Moon Battle Axe requires a Battle Axe +6 Moonstones (25 shards, 9 chunks, and 1 pure), 580 Souls per upgrade (2,900 total), and 850 Souls to fully repair:

Requires: Adds Magic Attack and Magic Bonus., Greatest Attack Power if at weapon Requirement and Magic is less than 70.

_Level data not available._

##### Blessed

The Blessed Battle Axe requires a Battle Axe +6, Faintstones (25 shards, 9 chunks, and 1 pure), 580 Souls per upgrade (2,900 total), and 850 Souls to fully repair:

Requires: Adds Magic Attack, Faith Bonus, and HP regeneration., Greatest Attack Power if at weapon Requirement and Faith is greater than 70.

_Level data not available._

##### Dozer Axe

The Dozer Axe requires a Battle Axe +6, a Grey Demon's Soul , and 1,180 Souls. Trivia

Requires: Though a standard weapon for Boletarian Soldiers (Axe and Shield), they instead have a chance to drop either the Guillotine Axe or Great Axe.

_Level data not available._

#### Bramd

> A dreadfully enormous coarse-iron hammer. One of Vinland's treasures. It's distortion is the mark of an old giant killer, and it increases the wielder's resistance to plague and poison. Even so, it is difficult to use even with both hands if the wielder has normal human strength.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 210/0/0 (Blunt) |
| **Durability** | **950** |
| **Weight** | 36.0 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 361/0/0/0 B/-/-/- |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 70.0/10.0 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 45 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

Like all Large Hammers, the attack animations of Bramd cannot be interrupted. Unfortunately, it has crushing weight, and less versatility than the Meat Cleaver . B-ranking in Strength is okay considering its hefty base damage, but there's no real reason to upgrade it besides improving its Poison and Plague resistance bonus. Has the highest durability of any weapon (and even any shield) in the game. It is the heaviest weapon (item) in the game. As such it has the highest stat requirement (36 STR) of any item in the game.

#### Meat Cleaver

> A large cleaver forged from the soul of the Demon "Adjudicator." It is the Adjudicator's own weapon, and transforms sinners into delicious-looking cuts of meat. It also steals a small amount of HP from its target when attacking, reflecting the original Soul's avaricious nature.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 90/90/0 (Slashing) |
| **Durability** | **400** |
| **Weight** | 10.0 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 261/0/0/16 S/S/-/A |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 70.0/10.0 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 45 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

A very powerful weapon due to immense stat scaling, albeit very slow in use. It deals slashing damage, but is actually classified as a 'Large Hammer'. The Meat Cleaver is the only weapon in the game that has two damage bonuses based on a rating of S. The physical damage scales with the Strength and Dexterity stats, while the Magical damage scales with Faith . The Meat Cleaver can be enchanted or have Fire applied to it for further attack bonuses.

---

The attack speed of the Hands of God is significantly slower than the other fist weapons If you are using them as a pair therefore, you would need ten Colorless Demon's Soul in order to get them both to +5.

#### Compound Long Bow

> A large composite bow. Its use requires strength but has higher power. Ranged weapons require the equipping of arrows as well as a bow. Also, from the position of readying an arrow, an aimed shot is possible with [L1] .

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 45/0 |
| **Range** | 60 |
| **Durability** | **100** |
| **Weight** | 2.0 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 221/12/0/0 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

The Quality bow or Hardstone +10 bow upgraded is more powerful for a character with a low Dexterity level, but as a character's Dexterity level increases (roughly, as it approaches 30 or so), the Sticky bow's Dexterity bonus makes it the most powerful bow in the game (that's comparing Sticky Compound Long +5 vs Compound Long +10, Quality Compound Long +5, White +5, and Lava bows). Combined with Heavy Arrows or White Arrows, any of these three +5 bows can inflict massive damage in a short amount of time. #### White Bow

> A long bow made of gnarled wood. With two intertwining strings, it is a legendary weapon said to be impossible for a human to handle. It has extremely long range.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 100/0 |
| **Range** | 75 |
| **Durability** | **300** |
| **Weight** | 1.5 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 301/24/0/0 E/E/-/- |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

The White Bow is extremely good if you have the stats for it in your first game or even NG+. While a Sticky Compound Long Bow +5 has a higher base damage for Dexterity-based characters, the White Bow has a longer range. Note: While appearing not have any benefits from upgrading, the White Bow does receive greater bonuses from STR and DEX with each upgrade. The marginal bonus damage is fairly small however as displayed by the fact it's scaling doesn't change. Image Name Damage Range Durability Weight Stats Needed Stat Bonuses White Bow 100/0 75 300 1.5 30 1 /24/0/0 E/E/-/-

---

#### Lava Bow

> A long bow with limbs like a spider's leg. It was forged from the Soul of the Demon "Armor Spider." It imbues all arrows fired with fire attributes.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 55/0 |
| **Range** | 55 |
| **Durability** | **400** |
| **Weight** | 2.0 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 211/16/0/0 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

While not listed in its stats, the Lava Bow applies an additional 100 Fire damage to all arrows it fires. This fire damage scales with the Magic stat. Preferred by low Dexterity players as it does not have high stat requirements, but still does good damage because of the passive fire effect. This will stun a great majority of the enemies for a small amount of time, allowing you to normally fire off a second shot before the enemy is able to move. This can be helpful in many situations, such as the Reapers in 4-2 . It is truly an exceptional bow for the first game and possibly even a new game+ or ++. This bow can also be great for PVP especially in 1-2 or 1-4 . This is mainly because of the dragons, but other natural hazards work just as well. Due to the short stun, an enemy hit by the Lava Bow will be more susceptible to other attacks, both from you or environmental hazards. For instance, you can hit enemies in time with one of the dragon's flame bursts. Other dangerous areas to take advantage of include 3-1 in front of the arrow walkway and 5-2 in the poison. Has the highest durability of any bow/crossbow in the game Image Name Damage Range Durability Weight Stats Needed Stat Bonuses Lava Bow 55/0 55 400 2.0 21 1 /16/0/0 D/D/E/-

#### Talisman of Beasts

> An old wooden amulet resembling the Old One. It can utilize both miracles and spells. The symbol of God was nothing more than the Image of the Old One.

| Stat | Value |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

The Talisman of Beasts is a multifunction item which serves as a Catalyst and Talisman at the same time. It is convenient to cast Miracle and Magic spells without having to switch between a Catalyst and Talisman and does of course leave a weapon slot available should you need use both types of Spell. It is the second strongest in Magic Power and in most cases it will do more damage with Spells than anything but the Insanity Catalyst . Miracles which don't rely on a stat like Second Chance or Evacuate are especially useful when the minimum Faith stat for the weapon is maintained. #### Istarelle

> A short spear of hard oak. It is covered with engravings and is one of the few Revelations from God. Once belonged to the Holy Knight Lizaia. Its holiness increases the wielder's resistance to plague and poison.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 20/100/0 (Piercing) |
| **Durability** | **800** |
| **Weight** | 1.0 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 9*/18/0/24 E/D/-/A |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 40.0/10.0 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 25 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

One of two unique weapons that scale amazingly well with Faith. The other being the Large Sword of Moonlight . Some Faith builds favor this spear over the Large Sword of Moonlight due to the fact that as a spear and is more adaptable, since you can 2-hand it to maximise damage or use a off-hand shield and defend yourself while attacking the enemy. The Istarelle multiplies Poison and Plague resistance from stats (Endurance and Luck, respectively) plus any bonus from a ring by a multiplier that starts at 3 and increases to 6 at the +5 upgraded version. While the bonus stacks very effectively with the Ring of Poison Resistance and Ring of Disease Resistance . The multiplier does not affect poison and plague resistance obtained from armor. At max stats the Istarelle will have 20 + 7 Physical Damage and 100 + 200 Magical Damage.

---

#### Scraping Spear

> A long spear with countless thorns on the tip. It was forged from the Soul of the Demon "Phalanx." It scrapes away at an opponent's equipment and lowers its durability.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 100/0/0 (Piercing) |
| **Durability** | **400** |
| **Weight** | 4.0 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 15*/15/0/0 E/A/-/- |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 40.0/10.0 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 25 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

While this weapon's damage output is low compared to other weapons obtainable with similar effort, it is unique in that every hit that lands (even if the enemy blocks it) does 10 durability damage to each item the target has equipped. When the durability of a weapon or piece of armor is below 30%, its effectiveness decreases greatly. Because of the special effect, this weapon is very effective against players that wield katanas, since it only takes 5 or so hits before their weapon durability is below the 30% point.

#### Phosphorescent Pole

> A long rod inlaid with a blue jewel that emits phosphorescent light. Bladeless and lightweight, and due to a magical magical enhancement, it slowly regenerates the wielder's MP. One of Lord Rydyell's most storied exploits is his theft of this weapon from the witch in the sky. 1

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

The MP regen of the Phosphorescent Pole; of any upgrade level, stacks with the MP regen of Crescent weapons and/or the Fragrant Ring . Two Phosphorescent Poles used simultaneously does lead to the combination of their MP regen rates. Provided the poles have different upgrade levels. For instance a +3 and +5 pole will provide a combined MP regen rate. Two +5 poles will not.

#### Adjudicator's Shield

> A large wooden shield that depicts a brightly colored scene of the deceased being judged. On the other side, an epigram is carved in old script: 'Cowardly acts and the eating of birds must not be the deeds of a Hero of Storms. If the one being judged displeases the Adjudicator's master, the Golden Crow, the deceased soul will be gnawed upon until nothing but their bones remain.' Its user will recover HP a little at a time.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 60/0/0 (Blunt) |
| **Durability** | **150** |
| **Weight** | 3.5 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 14/0/0/0 D/-/-/- |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 90/50/10 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 50 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

This shield is designed after a pavise shield. Though Adjudicator's Shield offers poor damage mitigation, its wielder will slowly regenerate HP. Thus, the shield is a good choice for anyone who prefers to wield weapons two-handed. It stacks with Blessed weapons and the Regenerator's Ring , which together will give significant HP regeneration. It can also be paired with the Magic Sword "Makoto" or the spell Cursed Weapon to offset the HP drain.

#### Tower Shield

> A gigantic shield used by one of the Boletarian heroes, the Tower Knight. It is said to repel all kinds of malice, but it is tremendously heavy and difficult to handle. It is considered the largest of the known types of shields.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 100/0/0 (Blunt) |
| **Durability** | **600** |
| **Weight** | 30.0 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 30/0/0/0 D/-/-/- |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 100/60/90 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 70 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

In terms of raw mitigation, this shield has it all: 100 Physical Damage Reduction 60 Magic Damage Reduction 90 Fire Damage Reduction 85 Guard Break Reduction when fully upgraded. In terms of practicality, however, this shield isn't quite there: Weighing in at a whopping 30 units, it is the second 1 heaviest piece of equipment in the entire game. Many players, especially on their first playthrough, will require the Ring of Great Strength to even consider using this shield. However, its defensive prowess is undeniable and higher-level characters with greater Endurance scores will enjoy the protection of this shield.

#### Dark Silver Shield

> The dark silver shield of the Vinland Legacy. Known as the oldest of metals, the dark silver is said to purify dark malice. It protects the user from all magics.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 70/0/0 |
| **Durability** | **450** |
| **Weight** | 5.0 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 16/0/0/0 D/-/-/- |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 100/100/70 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 50 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

Increases Magic defense. Decreases Magic damage. Magic damage penalty only affects Spells Blessed , Moon , Crescent and all weapons with Magic damage will not suffer any Magic damage penalty 1 Penalty to Magic Damage does not affect Faith Spells such as God's Wrath . Generates the same aura as the Rune Shield , Rune Sword and the Ring of Magical Dullness . The Dark Silver Shield tends to replace other auras with its own. This can be confusing at times, but your buffs are still in effect.

#### Large Brushwood Shield

> An old large shield owned by the Boletaria Royal Family from long ago. Particularly good at fending off flame type attacks. In recent times, it is known as an item of Biorr of the Twin Fangs.

| Stat | Value |
|------|-------|
| **Damage** | 80/0/0 (Blunt) |
| **Durability** | **600** |
| **Weight** | 13.0 |
| **Stats Needed / Bonuses** | 26/0/0/0 D/-/-/- |
| **Damage Reduction %** | 100/30/100 |
| **Guard Break Reduction** | 60 |

**Characteristics & Special Effects:**

While it is a little heavy, it offers 100% Damage Reduction against both Physical and Fire damage, and can be upgraded to 80 Guard Break Reduction through Colorless Demon's Souls . It offers mediocre Magic damage reduction, so carrying a spare shield with Magic protection is recommended. This shield is a marginal upgrade compared to the Purple Flame Shield . The Brushwood offers 10% more Fire damage Reduction, but weighs 5.0 units more and has slightly inferior Guard Break Reduction 2 , assuming both shields are maxed out. Depending on the player's priorities, however, this shield is arguably easier to fully upgrade.
